"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
} ],
"stream" : false

So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {

"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"

"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"

"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
}, {
"role" : "assistant",
"content" : "-- Creating instances of Currency\n!new Currency('currency1')\n!currency1.name := 'United States Dollar'\n!currency1.abbreviation := 'USD'\n\n-- Creating instances of Bills\n!new Bill('bill1')\n!bill1.amount := 50.00\n!bill1.category := 'Utilities'\n!bill1.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill1.paymentMethod := #CREDIT_CARD\n!bill1.status := #VERIFIED\n!insert (bill1, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill2')\n!bill2.amount := 30.00\n!bill2.category := 'Groceries'\n!bill2.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill2.paymentMethod := #CASH\n!bill2.status := #PENDING\n!insert (bill2, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill3')\n!bill3.amount := 20.00\n!bill3.category := 'Entertainment'\n!bill3.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill3.paymentMethod := #CHECK\n!bill3.status := #VALIDATED\n!insert (bill3, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n-- Creating instances of Comment\n!new Comment('comment1')\n!comment1.text := 'This expense is for monthly needs.'\n\n!new Comment('comment2')\n!comment2.text := 'Check payment needs confirmation.'\n\n-- Creating instance of Expense\n!new Expense('expense1')\n!expense1.amount := 100.00\n!expense1.startDate := Date('2023-09-01')\n!expense1.endDate := Date('2023-09-30')\n!insert (expense1, bill1) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill2) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill3) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, comment1) into ExpenseComment\n!insert (expense1, comment2) into ExpenseComment"

"role" : "system",
"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
}, {
"role" : "assistant",
"content" : "-- Creating instances of Currency\n!new Currency('currency1')\n!currency1.name := 'United States Dollar'\n!currency1.abbreviation := 'USD'\n\n-- Creating instances of Bills\n!new Bill('bill1')\n!bill1.amount := 50.00\n!bill1.category := 'Utilities'\n!bill1.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill1.paymentMethod := #CREDIT_CARD\n!bill1.status := #VERIFIED\n!insert (bill1, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill2')\n!bill2.amount := 30.00\n!bill2.category := 'Groceries'\n!bill2.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill2.paymentMethod := #CASH\n!bill2.status := #PENDING\n!insert (bill2, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill3')\n!bill3.amount := 20.00\n!bill3.category := 'Entertainment'\n!bill3.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill3.paymentMethod := #CHECK\n!bill3.status := #VALIDATED\n!insert (bill3, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n-- Creating instances of Comment\n!new Comment('comment1')\n!comment1.text := 'This expense is for monthly needs.'\n\n!new Comment('comment2')\n!comment2.text := 'Check payment needs confirmation.'\n\n-- Creating instance of Expense\n!new Expense('expense1')\n!expense1.amount := 100.00\n!expense1.startDate := Date('2023-09-01')\n!expense1.endDate := Date('2023-09-30')\n!insert (expense1, bill1) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill2) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill3) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, comment1) into ExpenseComment\n!insert (expense1, comment2) into ExpenseComment"

"messages" : [ {
"role" : "system",
"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
}, {
"role" : "assistant",
"content" : "-- Creating instances of Currency\n!new Currency('currency1')\n!currency1.name := 'United States Dollar'\n!currency1.abbreviation := 'USD'\n\n-- Creating instances of Bills\n!new Bill('bill1')\n!bill1.amount := 50.00\n!bill1.category := 'Utilities'\n!bill1.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill1.paymentMethod := #CREDIT_CARD\n!bill1.status := #VERIFIED\n!insert (bill1, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill2')\n!bill2.amount := 30.00\n!bill2.category := 'Groceries'\n!bill2.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill2.paymentMethod := #CASH\n!bill2.status := #PENDING\n!insert (bill2, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill3')\n!bill3.amount := 20.00\n!bill3.category := 'Entertainment'\n!bill3.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill3.paymentMethod := #CHECK\n!bill3.status := #VALIDATED\n!insert (bill3, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n-- Creating instances of Comment\n!new Comment('comment1')\n!comment1.text := 'This expense is for monthly needs.'\n\n!new Comment('comment2')\n!comment2.text := 'Check payment needs confirmation.'\n\n-- Creating instance of Expense\n!new Expense('expense1')\n!expense1.amount := 100.00\n!expense1.startDate := Date('2023-09-01')\n!expense1.endDate := Date('2023-09-30')\n!insert (expense1, bill1) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill2) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill3) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, comment1) into ExpenseComment\n!insert (expense1, comment2) into ExpenseComment"

"messages" : [ {
"role" : "system",
"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
}, {
"role" : "assistant",
"content" : "-- Creating instances of Currency\n!new Currency('currency1')\n!currency1.name := 'United States Dollar'\n!currency1.abbreviation := 'USD'\n\n-- Creating instances of Bills\n!new Bill('bill1')\n!bill1.amount := 50.00\n!bill1.category := 'Utilities'\n!bill1.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill1.paymentMethod := #CREDIT_CARD\n!bill1.status := #VERIFIED\n!insert (bill1, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill2')\n!bill2.amount := 30.00\n!bill2.category := 'Groceries'\n!bill2.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill2.paymentMethod := #CASH\n!bill2.status := #PENDING\n!insert (bill2, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill3')\n!bill3.amount := 20.00\n!bill3.category := 'Entertainment'\n!bill3.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill3.paymentMethod := #CHECK\n!bill3.status := #VALIDATED\n!insert (bill3, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n-- Creating instances of Comment\n!new Comment('comment1')\n!comment1.text := 'This expense is for monthly needs.'\n\n!new Comment('comment2')\n!comment2.text := 'Check payment needs confirmation.'\n\n-- Creating instance of Expense\n!new Expense('expense1')\n!expense1.amount := 100.00\n!expense1.startDate := Date('2023-09-01')\n!expense1.endDate := Date('2023-09-30')\n!insert (expense1, bill1) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill2) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill3) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, comment1) into ExpenseComment\n!insert (expense1, comment2) into ExpenseComment"

"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {

"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
}, {

"messages" : [ {
"role" : "system",
"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
}, {
"role" : "assistant",
"content" : "-- Creating instances of Currency\n!new Currency('currency1')\n!currency1.name := 'United States Dollar'\n!currency1.abbreviation := 'USD'\n\n-- Creating instances of Bills\n!new Bill('bill1')\n!bill1.amount := 50.00\n!bill1.category := 'Utilities'\n!bill1.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill1.paymentMethod := #CREDIT_CARD\n!bill1.status := #VERIFIED\n!insert (bill1, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill2')\n!bill2.amount := 30.00\n!bill2.category := 'Groceries'\n!bill2.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill2.paymentMethod := #CASH\n!bill2.status := #PENDING\n!insert (bill2, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill3')\n!bill3.amount := 20.00\n!bill3.category := 'Entertainment'\n!bill3.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill3.paymentMethod := #CHECK\n!bill3.status := #VALIDATED\n!insert (bill3, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n-- Creating instances of Comment\n!new Comment('comment1')\n!comment1.text := 'This expense is for monthly needs.'\n\n!new Comment('comment2')\n!comment2.text := 'Check payment needs confirmation.'\n\n-- Creating instance of Expense\n!new Expense('expense1')\n!expense1.amount := 100.00\n!expense1.startDate := Date('2023-09-01')\n!expense1.endDate := Date('2023-09-30')\n!insert (expense1, bill1) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill2) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill3) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, comment1) into ExpenseComment\n!insert (expense1, comment2) into ExpenseComment"

"model" : "openai/gpt-4o",
"messages" : [ {
"role" : "system",
"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
}, {
"role" : "assistant",
"content" : "-- Creating instances of Currency\n!new Currency('currency1')\n!currency1.name := 'United States Dollar'\n!currency1.abbreviation := 'USD'\n\n-- Creating instances of Bills\n!new Bill('bill1')\n!bill1.amount := 50.00\n!bill1.category := 'Utilities'\n!bill1.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill1.paymentMethod := #CREDIT_CARD\n!bill1.status := #VERIFIED\n!insert (bill1, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill2')\n!bill2.amount := 30.00\n!bill2.category := 'Groceries'\n!bill2.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill2.paymentMethod := #CASH\n!bill2.status := #PENDING\n!insert (bill2, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill3')\n!bill3.amount := 20.00\n!bill3.category := 'Entertainment'\n!bill3.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill3.paymentMethod := #CHECK\n!bill3.status := #VALIDATED\n!insert (bill3, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n-- Creating instances of Comment\n!new Comment('comment1')\n!comment1.text := 'This expense is for monthly needs.'\n\n!new Comment('comment2')\n!comment2.text := 'Check payment needs confirmation.'\n\n-- Creating instance of Expense\n!new Expense('expense1')\n!expense1.amount := 100.00\n!expense1.startDate := Date('2023-09-01')\n!expense1.endDate := Date('2023-09-30')\n!insert (expense1, bill1) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill2) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill3) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, comment1) into ExpenseComment\n!insert (expense1, comment2) into ExpenseComment"

00:01:44.893 [main] INFO  dev.langchain4j.http.client.log.LoggingHttpClient - HTTP request:
- method: POST
- url: https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions
- headers: [Authorization: Beare...3d], [User-Agent: langchain4j-openai], [Content-Type: application/json]
- body: {
"model" : "openai/gpt-4o",
"messages" : [ {
"role" : "system",
"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"

"model" : "openai/gpt-4o",
"messages" : [ {
"role" : "system",
"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
}, {
"role" : "assistant",
"content" : "-- Creating instances of Currency\n!new Currency('currency1')\n!currency1.name := 'United States Dollar'\n!currency1.abbreviation := 'USD'\n\n-- Creating instances of Bills\n!new Bill('bill1')\n!bill1.amount := 50.00\n!bill1.category := 'Utilities'\n!bill1.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill1.paymentMethod := #CREDIT_CARD\n!bill1.status := #VERIFIED\n!insert (bill1, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill2')\n!bill2.amount := 30.00\n!bill2.category := 'Groceries'\n!bill2.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill2.paymentMethod := #CASH\n!bill2.status := #PENDING\n!insert (bill2, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill3')\n!bill3.amount := 20.00\n!bill3.category := 'Entertainment'\n!bill3.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill3.paymentMethod := #CHECK\n!bill3.status := #VALIDATED\n!insert (bill3, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n-- Creating instances of Comment\n!new Comment('comment1')\n!comment1.text := 'This expense is for monthly needs.'\n\n!new Comment('comment2')\n!comment2.text := 'Check payment needs confirmation.'\n\n-- Creating instance of Expense\n!new Expense('expense1')\n!expense1.amount := 100.00\n!expense1.startDate := Date('2023-09-01')\n!expense1.endDate := Date('2023-09-30')\n!insert (expense1, bill1) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill2) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill3) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, comment1) into ExpenseComment\n!insert (expense1, comment2) into ExpenseComment"
}, {

"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
}, {
"role" : "assistant",
"content" : "-- Creating instances of Currency\n!new Currency('currency1')\n!currency1.name := 'United States Dollar'\n!currency1.abbreviation := 'USD'\n\n-- Creating instances of Bills\n!new Bill('bill1')\n!bill1.amount := 50.00\n!bill1.category := 'Utilities'\n!bill1.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill1.paymentMethod := #CREDIT_CARD\n!bill1.status := #VERIFIED\n!insert (bill1, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill2')\n!bill2.amount := 30.00\n!bill2.category := 'Groceries'\n!bill2.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill2.paymentMethod := #CASH\n!bill2.status := #PENDING\n!insert (bill2, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill3')\n!bill3.amount := 20.00\n!bill3.category := 'Entertainment'\n!bill3.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill3.paymentMethod := #CHECK\n!bill3.status := #VALIDATED\n!insert (bill3, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n-- Creating instances of Comment\n!new Comment('comment1')\n!comment1.text := 'This expense is for monthly needs.'\n\n!new Comment('comment2')\n!comment2.text := 'Check payment needs confirmation.'\n\n-- Creating instance of Expense\n!new Expense('expense1')\n!expense1.amount := 100.00\n!expense1.startDate := Date('2023-09-01')\n!expense1.endDate := Date('2023-09-30')\n!insert (expense1, bill1) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill2) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill3) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, comment1) into ExpenseComment\n!insert (expense1, comment2) into ExpenseComment"

00:02:04.303 [main] INFO  dev.langchain4j.http.client.log.LoggingHttpClient - HTTP request:
- method: POST
- url: https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions
- headers: [Authorization: Beare...3d], [User-Agent: langchain4j-openai], [Content-Type: application/json]
- body: {
"model" : "openai/gpt-4o",
"messages" : [ {
"role" : "system",
"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
}, {
"role" : "assistant",
"content" : "-- Creating instances of Currency\n!new Currency('currency1')\n!currency1.name := 'United States Dollar'\n!currency1.abbreviation := 'USD'\n\n-- Creating instances of Bills\n!new Bill('bill1')\n!bill1.amount := 50.00\n!bill1.category := 'Utilities'\n!bill1.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill1.paymentMethod := #CREDIT_CARD\n!bill1.status := #VERIFIED\n!insert (bill1, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill2')\n!bill2.amount := 30.00\n!bill2.category := 'Groceries'\n!bill2.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill2.paymentMethod := #CASH\n!bill2.status := #PENDING\n!insert (bill2, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill3')\n!bill3.amount := 20.00\n!bill3.category := 'Entertainment'\n!bill3.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill3.paymentMethod := #CHECK\n!bill3.status := #VALIDATED\n!insert (bill3, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n-- Creating instances of Comment\n!new Comment('comment1')\n!comment1.text := 'This expense is for monthly needs.'\n\n!new Comment('comment2')\n!comment2.text := 'Check payment needs confirmation.'\n\n-- Creating instance of Expense\n!new Expense('expense1')\n!expense1.amount := 100.00\n!expense1.startDate := Date('2023-09-01')\n!expense1.endDate := Date('2023-09-30')\n!insert (expense1, bill1) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill2) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill3) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, comment1) into ExpenseComment\n!insert (expense1, comment2) into ExpenseComment"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Please generate another instance that is structurally and semantically different from the previous ones."

"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"

"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"

- body: {
"model" : "openai/gpt-4o",
"messages" : [ {
"role" : "system",
"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
}, {
"role" : "assistant",
"content" : "-- Creating instances of Currency\n!new Currency('currency1')\n!currency1.name := 'United States Dollar'\n!currency1.abbreviation := 'USD'\n\n-- Creating instances of Bills\n!new Bill('bill1')\n!bill1.amount := 50.00\n!bill1.category := 'Utilities'\n!bill1.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill1.paymentMethod := #CREDIT_CARD\n!bill1.status := #VERIFIED\n!insert (bill1, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill2')\n!bill2.amount := 30.00\n!bill2.category := 'Groceries'\n!bill2.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill2.paymentMethod := #CASH\n!bill2.status := #PENDING\n!insert (bill2, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill3')\n!bill3.amount := 20.00\n!bill3.category := 'Entertainment'\n!bill3.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill3.paymentMethod := #CHECK\n!bill3.status := #VALIDATED\n!insert (bill3, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n-- Creating instances of Comment\n!new Comment('comment1')\n!comment1.text := 'This expense is for monthly needs.'\n\n!new Comment('comment2')\n!comment2.text := 'Check payment needs confirmation.'\n\n-- Creating instance of Expense\n!new Expense('expense1')\n!expense1.amount := 100.00\n!expense1.startDate := Date('2023-09-01')\n!expense1.endDate := Date('2023-09-30')\n!insert (expense1, bill1) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill2) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill3) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, comment1) into ExpenseComment\n!insert (expense1, comment2) into ExpenseComment"
}, {

"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
}, {

"content" : "-- Creating instances of Currency\n!new Currency('currency1')\n!currency1.name := 'United States Dollar'\n!currency1.abbreviation := 'USD'\n\n-- Creating instances of Bills\n!new Bill('bill1')\n!bill1.amount := 50.00\n!bill1.category := 'Utilities'\n!bill1.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill1.paymentMethod := #CREDIT_CARD\n!bill1.status := #VERIFIED\n!insert (bill1, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill2')\n!bill2.amount := 30.00\n!bill2.category := 'Groceries'\n!bill2.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill2.paymentMethod := #CASH\n!bill2.status := #PENDING\n!insert (bill2, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill3')\n!bill3.amount := 20.00\n!bill3.category := 'Entertainment'\n!bill3.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill3.paymentMethod := #CHECK\n!bill3.status := #VALIDATED\n!insert (bill3, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n-- Creating instances of Comment\n!new Comment('comment1')\n!comment1.text := 'This expense is for monthly needs.'\n\n!new Comment('comment2')\n!comment2.text := 'Check payment needs confirmation.'\n\n-- Creating instance of Expense\n!new Expense('expense1')\n!expense1.amount := 100.00\n!expense1.startDate := Date('2023-09-01')\n!expense1.endDate := Date('2023-09-30')\n!insert (expense1, bill1) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill2) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill3) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, comment1) into ExpenseComment\n!insert (expense1, comment2) into ExpenseComment"

"model" : "openai/gpt-4o",
"messages" : [ {
"role" : "system",
"content" : "Given a conceptual model expressed in the UML-based Specification Environment (USE), your task is to generate valid and realistic instances that conform to the provided model.\n<requirements>\n- Instances must be syntactically correct according to the USE syntax_reference.\n- Avoid unnecessary comments and output the instance in plain text (i.e., not markdown).\n- Make sure instances fulfill all the model's constraints, and that multiplicities, relationships, and attributes are valid and realistic.\n- Provide multiple instances with diverse data values and structure.\n</requirements>\n\n<syntax_reference>\nHere there is a snippet showing how to create objects and set values in the specific .soil language required:\n-- This is a comment example\n\n-- Primitive data types:\n    -- Integer i.e. 1, 2, 3, etc.\n    -- Real i.e. 1.0, 21.89, 322.05556, etc.\n    -- Boolean i.e. true or false\n    -- String i.e. 'Hello World'\n\n-- You can create instances with the following syntax:\n!new <instance type>('<instance name>')\n-- Example:\n!new Client('client1')\n!new Store('store4')\n\n-- You can assign values to attributes for a created instance with the following syntax:\n!<instance name>.<attribute name> := <value>\n-- Example for different data types:\n!client1.clientId := 1 -- For Integer\n!client1.balance := 1123.45 -- For Real\n!client1.name := 'John' -- For Strings\n!store4.available := true -- For Boolean\n\n-- You can create associations between instances with the following syntax:\n!insert (<instance name1>, <instance name2>) into <association name>\n-- Example:\n!insert (client1, store4) into ClientStore\n\n-- Custom data types usage:\n    -- dataType Location\n    -- operations\n    --   Location(x : Real, y : Real)\n    --   some other operations\n    -- end\n-- You can create custom data types by calling the constructor directly; in this case, the constructor of Location requires two arguments: x and y of type Real. So it can be used as follows:\n!store4.location := Location(14.0, 289.0)\n\n-- Enums usage:\n    -- enum Type { Clothes, Shoes }\n-- Can be used as follows:\n!store4.type := #Clothes\n</syntax_reference>\n"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Create an instance for the following domain model:\n<domain_model>\nmodel MyExpenses\n\ndataType Date\noperations\n    Date(date : String)\nend\n\nenum PaymentMethod {\n    CREDIT_CARD,\n    CHECK,\n    CASH\n}\n\nenum BillStatus {\n    DRAFT,\n    PENDING,\n    VERIFIED,\n    VALIDATED,\n    REFUSED,\n    REFUNDED\n}\n\nclass Expense\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    startDate : Date\n    endDate : Date\nend\n\nclass Bill\nattributes\n    amount : Real\n    category : String\n    date : Date\n    paymentMethod : PaymentMethod\n    status : BillStatus\nend\n\nclass Comment\nattributes\n    text : String\nend\n\nclass Currency\nattributes\n    name : String\n    abbreviation : String\nend\n\n\ncomposition ExpenseContainsBills between\n    Expense [1]\n    Bill [*] role bills\nend\n\nassociation ExpenseComment between\n    Expense [1]\n    Comment [*] role comments\nend\n\nassociation BillCurrency between\n    Bill [1..*]\n    Currency [1] role currency\nend\n\nconstraints\ncontext Expense inv positiveAmount:\n    self.amount >= 0\n\ncontext Expense inv expenseSumOfBills:\n    self.amount = self.bills->collect(b : Bill | b.amount)->sum()\n</domain_model>\n"
}, {
"role" : "assistant",
"content" : "-- Creating instances of Currency\n!new Currency('currency1')\n!currency1.name := 'United States Dollar'\n!currency1.abbreviation := 'USD'\n\n-- Creating instances of Bills\n!new Bill('bill1')\n!bill1.amount := 50.00\n!bill1.category := 'Utilities'\n!bill1.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill1.paymentMethod := #CREDIT_CARD\n!bill1.status := #VERIFIED\n!insert (bill1, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill2')\n!bill2.amount := 30.00\n!bill2.category := 'Groceries'\n!bill2.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill2.paymentMethod := #CASH\n!bill2.status := #PENDING\n!insert (bill2, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill3')\n!bill3.amount := 20.00\n!bill3.category := 'Entertainment'\n!bill3.date := Date('2023-09-01')\n!bill3.paymentMethod := #CHECK\n!bill3.status := #VALIDATED\n!insert (bill3, currency1) into BillCurrency\n\n-- Creating instances of Comment\n!new Comment('comment1')\n!comment1.text := 'This expense is for monthly needs.'\n\n!new Comment('comment2')\n!comment2.text := 'Check payment needs confirmation.'\n\n-- Creating instance of Expense\n!new Expense('expense1')\n!expense1.amount := 100.00\n!expense1.startDate := Date('2023-09-01')\n!expense1.endDate := Date('2023-09-30')\n!insert (expense1, bill1) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill2) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, bill3) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense1, comment1) into ExpenseComment\n!insert (expense1, comment2) into ExpenseComment"
}, {
"role" : "user",
"content" : "Please generate another instance that is structurally and semantically different from the previous ones."
}, {
"role" : "assistant",
"content" : "-- Creating instances of Currency\n!new Currency('currency2')\n!currency2.name := 'Euro'\n!currency2.abbreviation := 'EUR'\n\n-- Creating instances of Bills\n!new Bill('bill4')\n!bill4.amount := 150.00\n!bill4.category := 'Travel'\n!bill4.date := Date('2023-10-05')\n!bill4.paymentMethod := #CREDIT_CARD\n!bill4.status := #VALIDATED\n!insert (bill4, currency2) into BillCurrency\n\n!new Bill('bill5')\n!bill5.amount := 50.00\n!bill5.category := 'Hotel'\n!bill5.date := Date('2023-10-06')\n!bill5.paymentMethod := #CASH\n!bill5.status := #REFUNDED\n!insert (bill5, currency2) into BillCurrency\n\n-- Creating instances of Comment\n!new Comment('comment3')\n!comment3.text := 'Travel expenses for business trip.'\n\n-- Creating instance of Expense\n!new Expense('expense2')\n!expense2.amount := 200.00\n!expense2.startDate := Date('2023-10-05')\n!expense2.endDate := Date('2023-10-10')\n!insert (expense2, bill4) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense2, bill5) into ExpenseContainsBills\n!insert (expense2, comment3) into ExpenseComment"
}, {
"role" : "user",

}, {
